FILE: tests\bazi\test_bazi_tool.py
================================================================================

from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BaziChart
from guanchen_v2.tools.bazi import build_calculate_bazi_tool


def test_calculate_bazi_tool_schema():
    """验证工具暴露给大模型的 Schema，确保必填项齐全，且没有泄漏排盘配置信息。"""
    mock_service = Mock()
    bazi_tool = build_calculate_bazi_tool(service=mock_service)

    # 1. 验证提供给模型的 Tool 名称
    assert bazi_tool.name == "calculate_bazi"

    schema = bazi_tool.args_schema.model_json_schema()
    properties = schema.get("properties", {})
    required = schema.get("required", [])

    # 2. 验证大模型必须要生成的必填字段
    expected_required = {"year", "month", "day", "hour", "minute", "gender"}
    for req_field in expected_required:
        assert req_field in properties
        assert req_field in required

    # 3. 验证存在但不强制生成的选填字段
    assert "birth_place_text" in properties
    assert "birth_place_text" not in required

    # 4. 严格断言：大模型绝不能看到或干预的系统级溯源及流派参数
    internal_fields = [
        "late_zi_sect",
        "yun_sect",
        "library",
        "library_version",
        "true_solar_time",
        "birth_place_affects_calculation",
    ]
    for internal in internal_fields:
        assert internal not in properties


def test_calculate_bazi_tool_successful_invocation():
    """验证 Tool 调用链路返回一个可序列化的 JSON 兼容字典对象。"""
    mock_service = Mock()
    mock_chart = Mock(spec=BaziChart)
    mock_chart.model_dump_json.return_value = '{"day_master": "甲"}'
    mock_service.calculate.return_value = mock_chart

    bazi_tool = build_calculate_bazi_tool(service=mock_service)

    assert bazi_tool.response_format == "content_and_artifact"

    # 验证底层函数确实返回了 (字符串，原实体对象) 元组
    content, artifact = bazi_tool.func(year=2000, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender="male")
    assert content == '{"day_master": "甲"}'
    assert artifact is mock_chart


def test_calculate_bazi_tool_missing_fields_rejected():
    """验证缺少字段时，LangChain 工具的 args_schema 会直接拦截，不会调用底层业务逻辑。"""
    mock_service = Mock()
    bazi_tool = build_calculate_bazi_tool(service=mock_service)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        # 故意缺失 hour 和 minute 等核心字段
        bazi_tool.invoke({"year": 2000, "month": 1, "day": 1, "gender": "male"})

    mock_service.calculate.assert_not_called()

FILE: tests\bazi\test_service.py

from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.bazi.exceptions import BaziAdapterError, BaziCalculationError
from guanchen_v2.bazi.service import BaziService
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender


def test_bazi_service_happy_path():
    """验证服务正常代理到 Adapter，并正确返回数据。"""
    mock_adapter = Mock()
    mock_chart = Mock()
    mock_adapter.calculate.return_value = mock_chart

    service = BaziService(adapter=mock_adapter)
    profile = BirthProfile(year=2000, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE)

    result = service.calculate(profile)
    assert result is mock_chart
    mock_adapter.calculate.assert_called_once_with(profile=profile)


def test_bazi_service_error_translation():
    """验证服务将 Adapter 异常正确地翻译为 Calculation 异常，并保留根因。"""
    mock_adapter = Mock()
    mock_adapter.calculate.side_effect = BaziAdapterError("Simulated Adapter Error")

    service = BaziService(adapter=mock_adapter)
    profile = BirthProfile(year=2000, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE)

    with pytest.raises(BaziCalculationError) as exc_info:
        service.calculate(profile)

    assert str(exc_info.value) == "八字排盘业务执行失败。"
    assert isinstance(exc_info.value.__cause__, BaziAdapterError)

================================================================================
FILE: tests\config\test_settings.py

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings


def test_settings_instantiation_with_valid_kwargs():
    """Case 1: 显式参数可以创建 Settings"""
    settings = Settings(
        _env_file=None,  # 隔离真实 .env，避免测试意外依赖本地配置
        deepseek_api_key="test-secret-key",
        deepseek_model="deepseek-chat",
    )
    # 验证 SecretStr 的正确提取
    assert settings.deepseek_api_key.get_secret_value() == "test-secret-key"
    assert settings.deepseek_model == "deepseek-chat"
    assert settings.app_env == "development"


def test_settings_missing_required_fields_fails(monkeypatch):
    """Case 2: 缺 required API Key / model 时失败"""
    monkeypatch.delenv("GUANCHEN_DEEPSEEK_API_KEY", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.delenv("GUANCHEN_DEEPSEEK_MODEL", raising=False)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Settings(_env_file=None)


def test_settings_type_coercion(monkeypatch):
    """Case 3: Environment String 能转成数值类型"""
    monkeypatch.setenv("GUANCHEN_DEEPSEEK_API_KEY", "test-key")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GUANCHEN_DEEPSEEK_MODEL", "deepseek-chat")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GUANCHEN_LLM_MAX_RETRIES", "4")

    settings = Settings(_env_file=None)
    assert settings.llm_max_retries == 4
    assert isinstance(settings.llm_max_retries, int)


def test_settings_invalid_temperature_fails():
    """Case 4: 非法 temperature 被拒绝"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Settings(
            _env_file=None,
            deepseek_api_key="test",
            deepseek_model="test",
            llm_temperature=3.0,  # 超出 le=2 限制
        )


def test_settings_invalid_retry_fails():
    """Case 5: 非法 retry 被拒绝"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Settings(
            _env_file=None,
            deepseek_api_key="test",
            deepseek_model="test",
            llm_max_retries=-1,  # 超出 ge=0 限制
        )


def test_settings_empty_api_key_fails():
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Settings(
            _env_file=None,
            deepseek_api_key="",
            deepseek_model="deepseek-chat",
        )


def test_settings_invalid_log_level_fails():
    """验证：非法的日志等级会被 Pydantic 拒绝（Literal 约束）。"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Settings(
            _env_file=None,
            deepseek_api_key="test-key",
            deepseek_model="test-model",
            log_level="POTATO",  # 不在预设的 Enum 范围内
        )

FILE: tests\dream\test_context.py

import pytest
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamContextStatus, DreamPatch, DreamElements, RealityBackground,
    DreamCorrection, DreamField, DreamTurnBoundary
)
from guanchen_v2.dream.dream_merge import start_new_dream, merge_dream_record
from guanchen_v2.dream.context import DreamContextService, classify_dream_boundary

@pytest.fixture
def mock_extractor():
    extractor = MagicMock()
    extractor.extract = AsyncMock(return_value=DreamPatch())
    return extractor

@pytest.fixture
def context_service(mock_extractor):
    return DreamContextService(extractor=mock_extractor)

def test_explicit_same_dream_negation_does_not_reset():
    """测试带有 '不是另一个梦' 等否定句时，不会触发错误的新梦标记"""
    result = classify_dream_boundary(
        "这不是另一个梦，是刚才那个梦的补充",
        has_current_dream=True,
    )
    assert result == DreamTurnBoundary.SAME

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_3_consecutive_turns_same_dream(context_service, mock_extractor):
    """连续 3 轮梦境补充（场景 -> 现实背景 -> 修正人物），不丢旧字段"""
    # 轮次 1：初始梦境
    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(scenes=["旧学校", "走廊"]))
    res1 = await context_service.apply_turn("我梦见在学校走廊", None)
    
    assert res1.status == DreamContextStatus.UPDATED
    assert "旧学校" in res1.record.elements.scenes
    
    # 轮次 2：补充现实压力（注意这里不会触发 raw_description 的追加）
    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch(background_additions=RealityBackground(stressors=["考试"]))
    res2 = await context_service.apply_turn("最近考试压力大", res1.record)
    
    assert res2.status == DreamContextStatus.UPDATED
    assert "旧学校" in res2.record.elements.scenes
    assert "考试" in res2.record.reality_background.stressors
    assert len(res2.record.raw_descriptions) == 1 
    
    # 轮次 3：错误修正
    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch(corrections=[
        DreamCorrection(field=DreamField.SCENES, old_value="旧学校", new_value="小学")
    ])
    res3 = await context_service.apply_turn("不是旧学校，是小学", res2.record)
    
    assert res3.status == DreamContextStatus.UPDATED
    assert "旧学校" not in res3.record.elements.scenes
    assert "小学" in res3.record.elements.scenes
    assert "考试" in res3.record.reality_background.stressors
    assert res3.record.dream_id == res1.record.dream_id

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_explicit_new_dream(context_service, mock_extractor):
    """明确的新梦标记应重启 DreamRecord 且 严格屏蔽旧上下文"""
    
    # 构建一个有脏数据的旧梦境
    old_record = merge_dream_record(
        start_new_dream(),
        DreamPatch(
            element_additions=DreamElements(
                people=["妈妈"],
                scenes=["旧学校"],
            )
        ),
    )
    
    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(scenes=["海边"]))
    res = await context_service.apply_turn("换一个梦，我梦到了海边", old_record)
    
    assert res.status == DreamContextStatus.UPDATED
    assert res.started_new_dream is True
    assert res.record.dream_id != old_record.dream_id
    
    # 核心验证 1：Extractor 收到的 current_record 必须被完全置空
    kwargs = mock_extractor.extract.call_args.kwargs
    extraction_record = kwargs["current_record"]
    
    assert extraction_record.dream_id != old_record.dream_id
    assert extraction_record.elements.people == []
    assert extraction_record.elements.scenes == []
    
    # 核心验证 2：最终的 ContextResult 也绝对不能包含前一个梦的元素
    assert "妈妈" not in res.record.elements.people
    assert "旧学校" not in res.record.elements.scenes
    assert res.record.elements.scenes == ["海边"]

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ambiguous_dream_boundary_clarification(context_service):
    """边界模糊时不猜，直接触发澄清追问"""
    old_record = start_new_dream()
    res = await context_service.apply_turn("昨晚我又梦见在树林", old_record)
    
    assert res.status == DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
    assert "还是一个新的梦" in res.clarification_question

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_merge_conflict_clarification(context_service, mock_extractor):
    """明确修正但目标缺失时，转为澄清追问，绝不污染原状态"""
    old_record = start_new_dream()
    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch(corrections=[
        DreamCorrection(field=DreamField.PEOPLE, old_value="妈妈", new_value="同事")
    ])
    
    res = await context_service.apply_turn("不是妈妈，是同事", old_record)
    
    assert res.status == DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
    assert "哪一项信息" in res.clarification_question
    assert res.record == old_record

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_no_dream_content_clarification(context_service, mock_extractor):
    """仅包含现实压力，完全没有梦境实体时，触发补充追问"""
    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch(background_additions=RealityBackground(stressors=["加班"]))
    
    res = await context_service.apply_turn("最近疯狂加班", None)
    
    assert res.status == DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
    assert "哪怕只描述一两个最鲜明的片段" in res.clarification_question

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_empty_patch_then_what(context_service, mock_extractor):
    """'然后呢'导致的空补丁应正常延续状态，不引发无意义的强制问卷调查"""
    record = start_new_dream()
    record.elements.scenes.append("天台")
    
    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch()
    res = await context_service.apply_turn("然后呢？", record)
    
    assert res.status == DreamContextStatus.UPDATED
    assert res.started_new_dream is False
    assert "天台" in res.record.elements.scenes

================================================================================
FILE: tests\dream\test_dream_merge.py

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamRecord, 
    DreamPatch, 
    DreamElements, 
    RealityBackground, 
    DreamCorrection, 
    DreamField, 
    InterpretationStatus
)
from guanchen_v2.dream.exceptions import DreamMergeConflictError
from guanchen_v2.dream.dream_merge import merge_dream_record, start_new_dream, mark_interpreted

def test_consecutive_appends_retain_old_fields():
    record = start_new_dream()
    
    # Turn 1: Add Scene
    patch1 = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(scenes=["旧学校"]))
    record1 = merge_dream_record(record, patch1, raw_description_fragment="梦到在学校")
    assert "旧学校" in record1.elements.scenes
    
    # Turn 2: Add Person
    patch2 = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["姐姐"]))
    record2 = merge_dream_record(record1, patch2, raw_description_fragment="里面还有我姐姐")
    assert "旧学校" in record2.elements.scenes
    assert "姐姐" in record2.elements.people
    
    # Turn 3: Add Background (Stressors). 纯现实背景不写入 raw_descriptions.
    patch3 = DreamPatch(background_additions=RealityBackground(stressors=["最近工作压力大"]))
    record3 = merge_dream_record(record2, patch3)
    assert "旧学校" in record3.elements.scenes
    assert "姐姐" in record3.elements.people
    assert "最近工作压力大" in record3.reality_background.stressors
    
    # 证明第 3 轮未提供 raw 文本，因此 raw_descriptions 长度仍为 2
    assert len(record3.raw_descriptions) == 2

def test_merge_does_not_mutate_input_record():
    record = start_new_dream()
    record = merge_dream_record(
        record,
        DreamPatch(
            element_additions=DreamElements(
                scenes=["旧学校"],
                people=["姐姐"],
            )
        ),
    )

    original = record.model_copy(deep=True)

    patch = DreamPatch(
        element_additions=DreamElements(objects=["红门"]),
        corrections=[
            DreamCorrection(
                field=DreamField.PEOPLE,
                old_value="姐姐",
                new_value="同事",
            )
        ],
    )

    result = merge_dream_record(record, patch)

    # 断言 1：入参 record 完全没变
    assert record == original
    # 断言 2：返回的是全新对象内存地址
    assert result is not record
    # 断言 3：原来的 record 依然保留旧值
    assert record.elements.people == ["姐姐"]
    assert record.elements.objects == []

def test_reject_blank_structured_values():
    """测试 NonBlankText 能否有效拦截大模型可能输出的脏数据"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        DreamElements(people=["   "])
        
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        DreamCorrection(
            field=DreamField.PEOPLE, 
            old_value="", 
            new_value=None
        )

# ====== 以下为原有的其它测试，只需修改报错捕获的 match 文案 ======

def test_unmentioned_fields_remain_unchanged():
    record = start_new_dream()
    patch1 = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(scenes=["教室"], objects=["黑板"]))
    record1 = merge_dream_record(record, patch1)
    
    patch2 = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["老师"]))
    record2 = merge_dream_record(record1, patch2)
    
    assert record2.elements.scenes == ["教室"]
    assert record2.elements.objects == ["黑板"]
    assert record2.elements.people == ["老师"]

def test_stable_dedupe():
    record = start_new_dream()
    patch1 = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["同事"]))
    record1 = merge_dream_record(record, patch1)
    
    # Append the same concept with different spacing/case (simulate normalization target)
    patch2 = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=[" 同事 "]))
    record2 = merge_dream_record(record1, patch2)
    
    assert len(record2.elements.people) == 1
    assert record2.elements.people[0] == "同事"

def test_explicit_correction_replace():
    record = start_new_dream()
    record = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["姐姐"])))
    
    correction = DreamCorrection(
        field=DreamField.PEOPLE,
        old_value="姐姐",
        new_value="以前的同事"
    )
    record2 = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(corrections=[correction]))
    
    assert "姐姐" not in record2.elements.people
    assert "以前的同事" in record2.elements.people

def test_explicit_correction_remove():
    record = start_new_dream()
    record = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(objects=["红门"])))
    
    correction = DreamCorrection(
        field=DreamField.OBJECTS,
        old_value="红门",
        new_value=None
    )
    record2 = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(corrections=[correction]))
    
    assert "红门" not in record2.elements.objects
    assert len(record2.elements.objects) == 0

def test_invalid_correction_raises_conflict():
    record = start_new_dream()
    record = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["同事"])))
    
    correction = DreamCorrection(field=DreamField.PEOPLE, old_value="姐姐", new_value="路人")
    # match 文案已被修改为与核心逻辑中一致
    with pytest.raises(DreamMergeConflictError, match="当前梦境记录中不存在待修正目标"):
        merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(corrections=[correction]))

def test_interpretation_stale_logic():
    record = start_new_dream()
    record = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(scenes=["学校"])))
    
    # Simulate interpretation complete
    record = mark_interpreted(record)
    assert record.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.CURRENT
    
    # Empty patch (no-op) should not change revision or stale status
    record_noop = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch())
    assert record_noop.revision == record.revision
    assert record_noop.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.CURRENT
    
    # Effective change should bump revision and stale the status
    record_changed = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["同事"])))
    assert record_changed.revision == record.revision + 1
    assert record_changed.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.STALE

def test_new_dream_reset():
    old_record = start_new_dream()
    old_record = merge_dream_record(
        old_record, 
        DreamPatch(
            element_additions=DreamElements(people=["妈妈"]),
            background_additions=RealityBackground(stressors=["考试"])
        ),
        raw_description_fragment="梦见考试"
    )
    old_record = mark_interpreted(old_record)
    
    new_record = start_new_dream()
    assert new_record.dream_id != old_record.dream_id
    assert new_record.elements.people == []
    assert new_record.reality_background.stressors == []
    assert new_record.raw_descriptions == []
    assert new_record.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.NOT_STARTED
    assert new_record.revision == 1

FILE: tests\dream\test_extractor.py

import pytest
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock
from langchain_core.messages import SystemMessage, HumanMessage

from guanchen_v2.models.dream import DreamPatch, DreamElements
from guanchen_v2.dream.exceptions import DreamExtractionError, ProjectError
from guanchen_v2.dream.dream_merge import start_new_dream, merge_dream_record
from guanchen_v2.prompts.dream_agent import DREAM_EXTRACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT
from guanchen_v2.dream.extractor import DreamExtractor

class FakeLLMProjectError(ProjectError):
    pass

@pytest.fixture
def fake_llm_client():
    client = MagicMock()
    # 模拟 LangChain 绑定 schema 的行为，返回自身以便链式调用
    client.with_structured_output.return_value = client 
    # 模拟 ainvoke 返回结构化数据
    client.ainvoke = AsyncMock(return_value=DreamPatch())
    return client

@pytest.fixture
def extractor(fake_llm_client):
    # 初始化时会调用 fake_llm_client.with_structured_output(DreamPatch)
    return DreamExtractor(model=fake_llm_client)

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_extract_returns_dream_patch_and_uses_schema(extractor, fake_llm_client):
    # 期望模型产出的结构
    expected = DreamPatch(
        element_additions=DreamElements(
            scenes=["旧学校"],
            emotions=["害怕"],
        )
    )
    fake_llm_client.ainvoke.return_value = expected

    result = await extractor.extract("我梦到回到旧学校，梦里很害怕")

    # 验证返回值完全对等
    assert result == expected

    # 验证在初始化时正确绑定了 Schema
    fake_llm_client.with_structured_output.assert_called_once_with(DreamPatch)

    # 验证 LLM Client 调用合约 (检查传入 ainvoke 的 messages 列表)
    messages = fake_llm_client.ainvoke.call_args.args[0]
    assert len(messages) == 2
    assert isinstance(messages[0], SystemMessage)
    assert isinstance(messages[1], HumanMessage)
    
    assert messages[0].content == DREAM_EXTRACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT
    assert "旧学校" not in messages[0].content # 系统指令与数据隔离
    assert "我梦到回到旧学校" in messages[1].content

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_empty_input_raises_error_without_calling_llm(extractor, fake_llm_client):
    with pytest.raises(DreamExtractionError, match="抽取输入不能为空"):
        await extractor.extract("   ")
    fake_llm_client.ainvoke.assert_not_called()

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_llm_failure_is_wrapped_and_chained(extractor, fake_llm_client):
    fake_llm_client.ainvoke.side_effect = FakeLLMProjectError("LLM API Timeout")
    
    with pytest.raises(DreamExtractionError, match="梦境信息结构化抽取失败") as exc_info:
        await extractor.extract("我梦见了一只猫")
        
    assert isinstance(exc_info.value.__cause__, FakeLLMProjectError)

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_context_selection_hides_raw_history(extractor, fake_llm_client):
    record = start_new_dream()
    record = merge_dream_record(
        record, 
        DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(scenes=["旧学校"])),
        raw_description_fragment="第一句话，啰嗦的历史信息，不该发给模型"
    )
    
    await extractor.extract("第二句话", current_record=record)
    
    # 获取调用时传入的 messages 列表
    messages = fake_llm_client.ainvoke.call_args.args[0]
    user_prompt = messages[1].content
    
    assert "旧学校" in user_prompt
    assert "elements" in user_prompt
    
    assert "啰嗦的历史信息" not in user_prompt
    assert "raw_descriptions" not in user_prompt
    assert "interpretation_state" not in user_prompt

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_extraction_does_not_mutate_state(extractor):
    record = start_new_dream()
    record = merge_dream_record(
        record, 
        DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["姐姐"]))
    )
    
    original = record.model_copy(deep=True)
    await extractor.extract("其实不是姐姐，是同事", current_record=record)
    
    assert record == original

================================================================================
FILE: tests\dream\test_retrieval.py

import pytest
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock
from uuid import uuid4

from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamRecord, DreamElements, RealityBackground, DreamRetrievalAspect
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import RetrievalResult, RetrievalFilter, KnowledgeDomain, SourceType
from guanchen_v2.dream.retrieval import (
    build_dream_retrieval_queries, 
    dedupe_retrieval_results, 
    DreamRetrievalService,
    DreamRetrievalEvidence
)

def create_mock_retrieval_result(chunk_id: str) -> RetrievalResult:
    return RetrievalResult(
        rank=1,
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id=str(uuid4()),
        domain=list(KnowledgeDomain)[0],
        source_title="mock_title",
        source_type=list(SourceType)[0],
        section="mock_section",
        text="mock content",
        rrf_score=0.9,
        rrf_rank=1,
        reranker_score=None,
        dense_rank=None,
        sparse_rank=None
    )

def test_query_builder_all_aspects():
    """Case 1: 验证正确选择三个方向并组装规范文本，不包含 raw/revision"""
    record = DreamRecord(
        elements=DreamElements(objects=["黑猫"], emotions=["害怕"]),
        # 修正 fixture 语义干扰，不用诊断倾向的词汇
        reality_background=RealityBackground(stressors=["考试压力"]),
        raw_descriptions=["不要把我放进检索文本里"]
    )
    
    queries = build_dream_retrieval_queries(record)
    assert len(queries) == 3
    
    aspects = {q.aspect: q.query for q in queries}
    
    assert "黑猫" in aspects[DreamRetrievalAspect.SYMBOL]
    assert "害怕" in aspects[DreamRetrievalAspect.EMOTION]
    assert "考试压力" in aspects[DreamRetrievalAspect.REALITY_CONTEXT]
    
    assert not any("不要把我放进检索文本里" in q.query for q in queries)

def test_query_builder_missing_aspects():
    """Case 2: 缺失方向不产生多余的冗余查询"""
    record = DreamRecord(
        elements=DreamElements(objects=["蛇"])
    )
    
    queries = build_dream_retrieval_queries(record)
    assert len(queries) == 1
    assert queries[0].aspect == DreamRetrievalAspect.SYMBOL

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_retrieval_service_calls_shared_service():
    """Case 3: 调用 Shared RetrievalService 并完整传递 Filter"""
    mock_shared_service = MagicMock()
    mock_result = create_mock_retrieval_result("chunk-1")
    mock_shared_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[mock_result])
    
    service = DreamRetrievalService(mock_shared_service)
    
    record = DreamRecord(
        elements=DreamElements(objects=["蛇"], emotions=["恐惧"])
    )
    
    # 修复：不再猜测 RetrievalFilter 的内部字段，直接 Mock 一个壳子用于透传测试
    test_filter = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalFilter)
    
    evidence = await service.retrieve(record, filters=test_filter)
    
    assert len(evidence) == 2
    assert mock_shared_service.retrieve.call_count == 2
    
    # 核心验证点：保证上层组装的 Filter 没有被丢弃或拦截，完美透传给底座
    for call in mock_shared_service.retrieve.call_args_list:
        assert call.kwargs["filters"] == test_filter
    
    for ev in evidence:
        assert ev.results[0].chunk_id == "chunk-1"

def test_stable_chunk_dedupe():
    """Case 4: 稳定的 chunk 去重测试，尊重出场顺序"""
    res_A = create_mock_retrieval_result("A")
    res_B = create_mock_retrieval_result("B")
    res_C = create_mock_retrieval_result("C")
    
    ev_symbol = DreamRetrievalEvidence(
        aspect=DreamRetrievalAspect.SYMBOL,
        query="q1",
        results=[res_A, res_B]
    )
    ev_emotion = DreamRetrievalEvidence(
        aspect=DreamRetrievalAspect.EMOTION,
        query="q2",
        results=[res_B, res_C] 
    )
    
    deduped = dedupe_retrieval_results([ev_symbol, ev_emotion])
    
    assert len(deduped) == 3
    assert [r.chunk_id for r in deduped] == ["A", "B", "C"]

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_empty_retrieval_is_valid():
    """Case 5: 空检索合法"""
    mock_shared_service = MagicMock()
    mock_shared_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[]) 
    
    service = DreamRetrievalService(mock_shared_service)
    record = DreamRecord(elements=DreamElements(objects=["独角兽"]))
    
    evidence = await service.retrieve(record)
    
    assert len(evidence) == 1
    assert evidence[0].results == []

import pytest
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock
from uuid import uuid4

from guanchen_v2.models.dream import DreamRecord, DreamPatch, DreamElements, InterpretationStatus
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import RetrievalResult, KnowledgeDomain, SourceType, Citation

from guanchen_v2.dream.runtime import DreamAgentRuntime, DreamInterpretationDraft, DreamAgentStatus, DreamResponseMode
from guanchen_v2.dream.dream_merge import start_new_dream

class FakeLLMProjectError(ProjectError):
    pass

@pytest.fixture
def mock_context_service():
    return MagicMock()

@pytest.fixture
def mock_retrieval_service():
    service = MagicMock()
    service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[])
    return service

    client = MagicMock()
    client.generate_structured = AsyncMock(return_value=DreamInterpretationDraft(
        traditional_view="古书上说...",
        psychological_context_view="结合压力来看..."
    ))
    return client

@pytest.fixture

    return DreamAgentRuntime(
        context_service=mock_context_service,
        retrieval_service=mock_retrieval_service,
        llm_client=mock_llm_client
    )

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_clarification_short_circuit(runtime, mock_context_service, mock_retrieval_service, mock_llm_client):
    record = start_new_dream()
    mock_context_service.apply_turn = AsyncMock(return_value=DreamContextResult(
        status=DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
        record=record,
        clarification_question="要不要再补充点？"
    ))
    
    result = await runtime.handle_turn("不明所以的话")
    
    assert result.status == DreamAgentStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
    assert result.clarification_question == "要不要再补充点？"
    mock_retrieval_service.retrieve.assert_not_called()
    mock_llm_client.generate_structured.assert_not_called()

    ))

    assert "initial" in user_prompt
    assert result.status == DreamAgentStatus.ANSWERED
    assert result.record.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.CURRENT

    assert len(result.citations) == 0

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_interpretation_failure_keeps_merged_state(runtime, mock_context_service, mock_llm_client):
    record = start_new_dream()
    record.elements.people.append("新添加的人物")
    record.revision = 3
    record.interpretation_state.status = InterpretationStatus.STALE 
    
    mock_context_service.apply_turn = AsyncMock(return_value=DreamContextResult(
        status=DreamContextStatus.UPDATED,
        record=record,
        patch=DreamPatch()
    ))
    
    mock_llm_client.generate_structured.side_effect = FakeLLMProjectError("OpenAI API Server Error")
    
    with pytest.raises(DreamInterpretationError, match="梦境解释生成失败"):
        await runtime.handle_turn("继续解释")
        
    assert "新添加的人物" in runtime._current_record.elements.people
    assert runtime._current_record.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.STALE

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_traditional_evidence_creates_citations_and_hides_scores(runtime, mock_context_service, mock_retrieval_service, mock_llm_client):
    """Case 7: 确保 Citation 由代码确定性生成，并且底层检索的分数元数据绝对不上 LLM 牌桌"""
    record = start_new_dream()
    mock_context_service.apply_turn = AsyncMock(return_value=DreamContextResult(
        status=DreamContextStatus.UPDATED,
        record=record,
        patch=DreamPatch()
    ))
    
    # 模拟底座真实返回的一个带分数的检索对象
    mock_result = RetrievalResult(
        rank=1,
        chunk_id="chunk-1",
        document_id="doc-1",
        domain=list(KnowledgeDomain)[0],
        source_title="周公解梦",
        source_type=list(SourceType)[0],
        section="动物篇",
        text="梦见蛇...",
        rrf_score=0.999,
        rrf_rank=1,
        reranker_score=None,
        dense_rank=1,
        sparse_rank=2
    )
    
    # 伪造 RetrievalService 返回
    evidence_groups = [
        DreamRetrievalEvidence(
            aspect=DreamRetrievalAspect.SYMBOL,
            query="梦境象征",
            results=[mock_result]
        )
    ]
    mock_retrieval_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=evidence_groups)
    
    result = await runtime.handle_turn("我梦见蛇")
    
    # 验证点 1：返回的 citations 必须精确匹配 RetrievalResult
    assert len(result.citations) == 1
    assert result.citations[0].chunk_id == "chunk-1"
    assert result.citations[0].document_id == "doc-1"
    assert result.citations[0].source_title == "周公解梦"
    assert result.citations[0].section == "动物篇"
    
    # 验证点 2：LLM User Prompt 里必须藏起所有的搜索打分
    user_prompt = mock_llm_client.generate_structured.call_args.kwargs["user_prompt"]
    assert "周公解梦" in user_prompt
    assert "梦见蛇..." in user_prompt
    assert "rrf_score" not in user_prompt
    assert "dense_rank" not in user_prompt
    assert "0.999" not in user_prompt

import math
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import numpy as np
import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings import BgeEmbeddingAdapter
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeEmbeddingError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeChunk, KnowledgeDomain, SourceType


def _create_dummy_chunk(chunk_id: str, title: str, section: str, text: str) -> KnowledgeChunk:
    # 使用 model_construct 绕过文本清洗以便专心测试 Adapter 容错
    return KnowledgeChunk.model_construct(
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id="doc1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title=title,
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section=section,
        text=text,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Mock 隔离测试区
# ---------------------------------------------------------


@patch("guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings.SentenceTransformer")
def test_model_initialization_failure(mock_st_class):
    """验证模型加载失败时抛出正确的项目异常，并保留底层根因"""
    mock_st_class.side_effect = RuntimeError("Disk full")
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeEmbeddingError, match="Embedding 模型加载失败") as exc_info:
        BgeEmbeddingAdapter()
    assert isinstance(exc_info.value.__cause__, RuntimeError)


@patch("guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings.SentenceTransformer")
def test_embed_documents_input_and_order_contract(mock_st_class):
    """核心防线：确保输入不乱序，且输出能够严格按位置对齐 mapping"""
    mock_model = MagicMock()
    mock_st_class.return_value = mock_model

    # 构造极具辨识度的数据以验证是否乱序：
    # Row 0 开头为 1.0, Row 1 开头为 2.0
    row_1 = np.full((512,), 1.0, dtype=np.float32)
    row_2 = np.full((512,), 2.0, dtype=np.float32)
    mock_model.encode.return_value = np.array([row_1, row_2])

    adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()
    chunks = [
        _create_dummy_chunk("c1", "《书1》", "第一章", "正文1"),
        _create_dummy_chunk("c2", "《书2》", "第二章", "正文2"),
    ]
    vectors = adapter.embed_documents(chunks)

    # 验证传入模型的参数
    call_args, _ = mock_model.encode.call_args
    texts_passed = call_args[0]
    assert texts_passed[0] == "《书1》\n第一章\n正文1"
    assert texts_passed[1] == "《书2》\n第二章\n正文2"

    # 验证输出的位置映射是否完美
    assert len(vectors) == 2
    assert vectors[0][0] == 1.0
    assert vectors[1][0] == 2.0


@patch("guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings.SentenceTransformer")
def test_embed_documents_fails_on_shape_mismatch(mock_st_class):
    """防御三方库诡异返回：比如只返回了一行，或者维度变成 768"""
    mock_model = MagicMock()
    mock_st_class.return_value = mock_model
    adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()
    chunks = [
        _create_dummy_chunk("c1", "书", "章", "正文"),
        _create_dummy_chunk("c2", "书", "章", "正文"),
    ]

    # 场景1：数量不对 (2个文本只返回1行)
    mock_model.encode.return_value = np.random.rand(1, 512)
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeEmbeddingError, match="形状异常，预期为 \\(2, 512\\)"):
        adapter.embed_documents(chunks)

    # 场景2：维度不对
    mock_model.encode.return_value = np.random.rand(2, 768)
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeEmbeddingError, match="形状异常，预期为 \\(2, 512\\)"):
        adapter.embed_documents(chunks)


@patch("guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings.SentenceTransformer")
def test_embed_documents_fails_on_empty_chunk_text(mock_st_class):
    adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()
    chunks = [_create_dummy_chunk("c1", "《书》", "章", "   ")]
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeEmbeddingError, match="尝试对空文本进行 Embedding"):
        adapter.embed_documents(chunks)


@patch("guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings.SentenceTransformer")
def test_embed_query_happy_path(mock_st_class):
    mock_model = MagicMock()
    mock_st_class.return_value = mock_model
    mock_model.encode.return_value = np.random.rand(512).astype(np.float32)

    adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()
    vector = adapter.embed_query("八字怎么看？")
    assert isinstance(vector, list)
    assert len(vector) == 512


@patch("guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings.SentenceTransformer")
def test_embed_query_fails_on_empty_or_shape_mismatch(mock_st_class):
    mock_model = MagicMock()
    mock_st_class.return_value = mock_model
    adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()

    # 场景1：空 Query
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeEmbeddingError, match="不能为空"):
        adapter.embed_query("   ")

    # 场景2：返回二维数组 (例如被错误地包裹了一层)
    mock_model.encode.return_value = np.random.rand(1, 512)
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeEmbeddingError, match="输出了错误的形状.*预期应为 \\(512,\\)"):
        adapter.embed_query("正当查询")


# ---------------------------------------------------------
# 真实模型 Smoke Test
# ---------------------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.smoke
def test_real_bge_model_smoke():
    """
    真实的模型推理测试。只在明确指定 uv run pytest -m smoke 时运行。
    任何网络/模型加载问题在此处都应该 FAIL，而不是静默 skip。
    """
    from guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings import BgeEmbeddingAdapter

    adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()

    chunks = [
        _create_dummy_chunk("1", "书", "章", "真实大模型推理测试 A"),
        _create_dummy_chunk("2", "书", "章", "真实大模型推理测试 B"),
    ]
    vectors = adapter.embed_documents(chunks)
    assert len(vectors) == 2
    assert len(vectors[0]) == 512

    norm = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in vectors[0]))
    assert math.isclose(norm, 1.0, rel_tol=1e-5)

    query_vector = adapter.embed_query("测试 Query")
    assert len(query_vector) == 512
    q_norm = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in query_vector))
    assert math.isclose(q_norm, 1.0, rel_tol=1e-5)


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\test_eval_metrics.py
================================================================================

import math

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.eval_metrics import (
    calculate_mrr_at_k,
    calculate_percentile,
    calculate_recall_at_k,
    calculate_relative_mrr_gain,
)


def test_recall_at_k_logic():
    gt = {"A", "B", "C"}
    assert calculate_recall_at_k(gt, ["X", "A", "Y", "C", "Z"], k=5) == 2 / 3
    assert calculate_recall_at_k(gt, ["X", "A", "Y", "C", "Z"], k=2) == 1 / 3
    assert calculate_recall_at_k(gt, ["X", "Y", "Z"], k=5) == 0.0

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="k 必须大于 0"):
        calculate_recall_at_k(gt, ["A"], k=0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="不能为空"):
        calculate_recall_at_k(set(), ["A"], k=5)


def test_mrr_at_k_logic():
    gt = {"A", "B"}
    assert calculate_mrr_at_k(gt, ["X", "Y", "A", "B"], k=5) == 1 / 3
    assert calculate_mrr_at_k(gt, ["B", "Y", "X", "A"], k=5) == 1.0
    assert calculate_mrr_at_k(gt, ["X", "Y", "A", "B"], k=2) == 0.0

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="k 必须大于 0"):
        calculate_mrr_at_k(gt, ["A"], k=-1)


def test_relative_mrr_gain():
    assert math.isclose(calculate_relative_mrr_gain(0.70, 0.735), 0.05, rel_tol=1e-5)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="baseline MRR 必须大于 0，才能计算 relative MRR gain"):
        calculate_relative_mrr_gain(0.0, 0.5)


def test_percentile_logic():
    latencies = [10.0, 20.0, 30.0, 40.0, 50.0]
    assert calculate_percentile(latencies, 50) == 30.0
    assert calculate_percentile(latencies, 95) > 45.0

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="没有 latency observation，无法计算 percentile"):
        calculate_percentile([], 95)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="percentile 必须位于"):
        calculate_percentile([10.0], 105)


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\test_models.py
================================================================================

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    KnowledgeChunk,
    KnowledgeDocument,
    KnowledgeDomain,
    SourceType,
    generate_deterministic_chunk_id,
)


def test_document_rejects_empty_text_or_whitespace():
    """测试文本规范：阻断纯空格或被剔除后为空的垃圾文本"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="不能为空"):
        KnowledgeDocument(
            document_id="doc_1",
            domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
            source_title="书籍",
            source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
            section="章一",
            text="   \n   \t  ",
        )


def test_deterministic_chunk_id_consistency():
    """核心：相同来源、章节和内容，必须产生完全一致的 ID。"""
    id1 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "这是一段相同的文本")
    id2 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "这是一段相同的文本")
    assert id1 == id2


def test_deterministic_chunk_id_change_awareness():
    """核心：内容发生变动（哪怕只是一个字）或源头改变，指纹必定改变"""
    base_id = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "一段文本")
    assert base_id != generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "一段文本，加了个字")
    assert base_id != generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec2", "一段文本")


def test_knowledge_chunk_from_document_part_happy_path():
    """验证官方构造路径能够无缝组合各类元数据，并正确挂载 Deterministic ID"""
    chunk = KnowledgeChunk.from_document_part(
        document_id="yuanhai_ziping__juan_01",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="《渊海子平》",
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section="卷一",
        text="   天干者，甲乙丙丁也。   \n\n  ",
    )

    # 元数据应清洗并透传
    assert chunk.document_id == "yuanhai_ziping__juan_01"
    assert chunk.domain == KnowledgeDomain.BAZI
    # 因为保存时只做基础 strip，不会压缩换行，所以 \n\n 应该被保全
    assert chunk.text == "天干者，甲乙丙丁也。"

    # ID 仍由底层的 canonicalize 计算得出，不受外层空格影响
    expected_id = generate_deterministic_chunk_id(
        "yuanhai_ziping__juan_01", "卷一", "天干者，甲乙丙丁也。   \n\n  "
    )
    assert chunk.chunk_id == expected_id


def test_chunk_id_whitespace_semantics():
    """验证 Chunk ID canonicalization 语义：纯空白符变化哈希不变，但有意义的分割保留"""
    # 这三者都被化简成 "甲 乙"，因此 ID 完全相同
    id1 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "甲   乙")
    id2 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "甲\t乙")
    id3 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "甲\n乙")
    assert id1 == id2 == id3

    # 原生单空格自然也等于化简后的单空格
    id4 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "甲 乙")
    assert id1 == id4

    # 但完全粘连的词汇是另一种语义，ID 必须不同！
    id5 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "甲乙")
    assert id1 != id5


================================================================================
FILE: tests\llm\test_client.py
================================================================================

from unittest.mock import Mock

import httpx
import pytest
from langchain_core.language_models.chat_models import BaseChatModel
from langchain_core.messages import AIMessage
from openai import APIStatusError
from pydantic import BaseModel

import guanchen_v2.llm.client
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.llm.exceptions import (
    LLMEmptyResponseError,
    LLMProviderError,
    LLMRetryExhaustedError,
    LLMStructuredOutputError,
)


class DummySchema(BaseModel):
    field: str


@pytest.fixture
def fake_model():
    return Mock(spec=BaseChatModel)


@pytest.fixture
def llm_client(fake_model):
    # 关闭 jitter 以保证测试执行极速且 deterministic
    return LLMClient(model=fake_model, max_retries=2, retry_wait_exponential_jitter=False)


def test_text_happy_path(llm_client, fake_model):
    """Case 1: text happy path"""
    fake_model.invoke.return_value = AIMessage(content="ok")

    result = llm_client.text("hello")

    assert result == "ok"
    assert fake_model.invoke.call_count == 1


def test_non_retryable_provider_failure(llm_client, fake_model):
    """Case 2: non-retryable provider failure (400 Bad Request)"""
    mock_request = httpx.Request("POST", "https://api.deepseek.com/chat")
    fake_model.invoke.side_effect = APIStatusError(
        message="Bad Request", response=httpx.Response(400, request=mock_request), body=None
    )

    with pytest.raises(LLMProviderError):
        llm_client.text("hello")

    # 验证非瞬时错误直接熔断，不会发起重试
    assert fake_model.invoke.call_count == 1


def test_empty_structured_output_exhausts_retries(llm_client, fake_model):
    """Case 3: empty structured output -> finite retries -> LLMEmptyResponseError"""
    structured_mock = Mock()
    structured_mock.invoke.return_value = None
    fake_model.with_structured_output.return_value = structured_mock

    with pytest.raises(LLMEmptyResponseError):
        llm_client.structured("hello", DummySchema)

    # 1次原始请求 + 2次重试 = 3次
    assert structured_mock.invoke.call_count == 3


def test_structured_parse_failure_exhausts_retries(llm_client, fake_model):
    """Case 4: structured parse failure -> finite retries -> LLMStructuredOutputError"""
    structured_mock = Mock()
    # 返回了普通的字典，而不是 DummySchema 实例，触发类型断言错误
    structured_mock.invoke.return_value = {"field": "wrong type"}
    fake_model.with_structured_output.return_value = structured_mock

    with pytest.raises(LLMStructuredOutputError):
        llm_client.structured("hello", DummySchema)

    assert structured_mock.invoke.call_count == 3


def test_transient_provider_failure_exhausts_retries(llm_client, fake_model):
    """Case 5: transient provider failure -> finite retries -> LLMRetryExhaustedError"""
    mock_request = httpx.Request("POST", "https://api.deepseek.com/chat")
    # 模拟 502 Bad Gateway
    fake_model.invoke.side_effect = APIStatusError(
        message="Bad Gateway", response=httpx.Response(502, request=mock_request), body=None
    )

    with pytest.raises(LLMRetryExhaustedError):
        llm_client.text("hello")

    assert fake_model.invoke.call_count == 3


def test_from_settings_delegates_to_builder(monkeypatch):
    """
    重构回归测试：验证 LLMClient.from_settings 确实将模型构造委托给了 provider 层，
    并保留了自身的应用级 retry 控制权。
    """
    # 1. 伪造一个 BaseChatModel 实例
    fake_model = Mock(spec=BaseChatModel)

    # 2. 拦截 client 模块中的 build_chat_model 函数引用
    mock_build_chat_model = Mock(return_value=fake_model)
    monkeypatch.setattr(guanchen_v2.llm.client, "build_chat_model", mock_build_chat_model)

    # 3. 伪造 Settings 以避免真实环境变量文件的干扰
    mock_settings = Mock()
    mock_settings.llm_max_retries = 3

    # 4. 执行被测构造方法
    client = LLMClient.from_settings(mock_settings)

    # 5. 断言验证：确实发生了一次无损的参数委托
    mock_build_chat_model.assert_called_once_with(settings=mock_settings)

    # 6. （可选的防御性断言）在 Python 中我们可以直接验证内部组装是否成功
    # 证明装配后的模型就是工厂吐出的模型，且保留了 settings 里的 retry 次数
    assert client._model is fake_model
    assert client._max_retries == 3


================================================================================
FILE: tests\llm\test_provider.py
================================================================================

from unittest.mock import Mock

import guanchen_v2.llm.provider
from guanchen_v2.llm.provider import build_chat_model


def test_build_chat_model_contract(monkeypatch):
    """
    验证基础模型工厂是否严格遵循 Stage 1 的构造契约。
    重点防御底层重试泄露 (max_retries=0) 和 thinking mode 禁用。
    """
    mock_chat_deepseek = Mock()
    monkeypatch.setattr(guanchen_v2.llm.provider, "ChatDeepSeek", mock_chat_deepseek)

    # 使用 Mock 对象彻底隔离 Pydantic Settings 对 .env 文件的磁盘读取
    mock_settings = Mock()
    mock_settings.deepseek_model = "deepseek-test"
    # llm_api_key 是 SecretStr，因此我们要 mock 它的 get_secret_value 方法
    mock_settings.deepseek_api_key.get_secret_value.return_value = "sk-fake-key"
    mock_settings.llm_temperature = 0.8
    mock_settings.llm_timeout_seconds = 45.0

    # 调用工厂
    build_chat_model(mock_settings)

    mock_chat_deepseek.assert_called_once()
    call_kwargs = mock_chat_deepseek.call_args.kwargs

    # 验证常规透传
    assert call_kwargs["model"] == "deepseek-test"
    assert call_kwargs["api_key"] == "sk-fake-key"
    assert call_kwargs["temperature"] == 0.8
    assert call_kwargs["timeout"] == 45.0

    # 验证底线契约
    assert call_kwargs["max_retries"] == 0
    assert call_kwargs["extra_body"].get("thinking", {}).get("type") == "disabled"


================================================================================
FILE: tests\models\test_bazi_models.py

from datetime import datetime

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import (
    BaziCalculationMethod,
    BaziChart,
    BirthProfile,
    DaYunPeriod,
    DaYunResult,
    EarthlyBranch,
    FiveElement,
    GanZhi,
    Gender,
    HeavenlyStem,
    HiddenStem,
    LuckDirection,
    LuckStartOffset,
    Pillar,
    TenGod,
)


def test_valid_birth_profile():
    profile = BirthProfile(
        year=2024,
        month=2,
        day=29,
        hour=14,
        minute=30,
        gender=Gender.MALE,
        birth_place_text="  成都  ",
    )
    assert profile.year == 2024
    assert profile.gender == Gender.MALE
    assert profile.birth_place_text == "成都"  # 验证清洗


def test_birth_profile_empty_place():
    profile = BirthProfile(
        year=2024, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE, birth_place_text="   "
    )
    assert profile.birth_place_text is None  # 空白字符串转为 None


def test_impossible_calendar_date():
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="非法的日期.*组合"):
        BirthProfile(year=2023, month=2, day=29, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.FEMALE)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="非法的日期.*组合"):
        BirthProfile(year=2025, month=2, day=30, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE)


def test_illegal_calculation_method():
    # 验证严格禁用的排盘规则会被拒绝
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        BaziCalculationMethod(
            library_version="1.0",
            late_zi_sect=1,
            yun_sect=1,
            true_solar_time=True,  # Stage 2 锁定禁止
        )


def test_empty_lists_rejected():
    gz = GanZhi(stem=HeavenlyStem.JIA, branch=EarthlyBranch.ZI)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Pillar(gan_zhi=gz, stem_ten_god=None, hidden_stems=[])  # 必须包含藏干

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        DaYunResult(
            direction=LuckDirection.FORWARD,
            start_offset=LuckStartOffset(years=0, months=0, days=0, hours=0),
            start_at=datetime(2030, 1, 1, 0, 0),
            periods=[],  # 必须包含大运步
        )


def test_derived_fields_and_invariants():
    # 验证甲子干支正确派生出五行为：水、木
    gz = GanZhi(stem=HeavenlyStem.JIA, branch=EarthlyBranch.ZI)
    assert gz.gan_zhi == "甲子"

    hidden = HiddenStem(stem=HeavenlyStem.GUI, ten_god=TenGod.ZHENG_YIN)
    assert hidden.element == FiveElement.WATER

    pillar = Pillar(gan_zhi=gz, stem_ten_god=None, hidden_stems=[hidden])
    assert pillar.stem_element == FiveElement.WOOD
    assert pillar.branch_element == FiveElement.WATER

    dummy_method = BaziCalculationMethod(library_version="1.0", late_zi_sect=2, yun_sect=1)
    dummy_dayun = DaYunResult(
        direction=LuckDirection.FORWARD,
        start_offset=LuckStartOffset(years=0, months=0, days=0, hours=0),
        start_at=datetime(2030, 1, 1, 0, 0),
        periods=[DaYunPeriod(index=1, start_year=2025, start_age=1, gan_zhi=gz)],
    )

    chart = BaziChart(
        year_pillar=pillar,
        month_pillar=pillar,
        day_pillar=pillar,
        hour_pillar=pillar,
        da_yun=dummy_dayun,
        calculation_method=dummy_method,
    )
    # 日主自动从日柱天干甲读取
    assert chart.day_master == HeavenlyStem.JIA


================================================================================
FILE: tests\agents\bazi\test_requirements.py
================================================================================

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziAgentInput,
    InvalidBaziInput,
    MissingField,
    NeedUserInput,
    ReadyForBazi,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.requirements import check_bazi_requirements
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import Gender


def test_complete_input_ready_preserves_question():
    """Case 1: 资料齐全，不仅得到 BirthProfile，原问题也一字不落地保留并清洗。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(
        question="  测事业  ", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE
    )
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, ReadyForBazi)
    assert result.question == "测事业"
    assert result.profile.year == 1998


def test_missing_time_needs_input_preserves_question():
    """Case 2: 缺时辰时分，触发 NeedUserInput，同时记住用户想问什么。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="运势如何？", year=1998, month=7, day=21, gender=Gender.MALE)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, NeedUserInput)
    assert result.original_question == "运势如何？"
    assert result.missing_fields == [MissingField.HOUR, MissingField.MINUTE]


def test_missing_gender_needs_input():
    """Case 3: 缺少性别，触发 NeedUserInput。绝对不调用 LLM 去猜。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="测财运", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, NeedUserInput)
    assert result.missing_fields == [MissingField.GENDER]


def test_multiple_missing_fields_stable_order():
    """Case 4: 大面积缺失资料，确保系统返回的 missing_fields 顺序是稳定的。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="我适合做什么？", year=2000)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, NeedUserInput)
    assert result.missing_fields == [
        MissingField.MONTH,
        MissingField.DAY,
        MissingField.HOUR,
        MissingField.MINUTE,
        MissingField.GENDER,
    ]


def test_invalid_calendar_date_preserves_question():
    """Case 5: 非法的跨字段日期组合，拦截器立刻阻断并报 Invalid。已知错误优先于缺失信息。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="算一卦", year=2023, month=2, day=29)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, InvalidBaziInput)
    assert result.original_question == "算一卦"
    assert "非法的日期组合" in result.reason


def test_empty_question_rejected():
    """Case 6: 最基础的输入 Schema 校验，拦截空白问题。"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="question 不能为空或纯空格"):
        BaziAgentInput(question="   ", year=1998, month=1, day=1)


def test_known_single_field_invalid_rejected():
    """Case 7: 单字段自身的硬伤（如 year=0）直接在模型层被拒收，不会流入 NeedUserInput。"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        BaziAgentInput(question="测事业", year=0, month=1, day=1)

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

import pytest
from langchain_core.messages import ToolMessage

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.exceptions import BaziAgentInvariantError
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import BaziInterpretation, ReadyForBazi
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime import BaziAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.bazi.adapter import LunarPythonBaziAdapter
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDomain, RetrievalResult, SourceType
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender
from guanchen_v2.tools.bazi_retrieval import BaziKnowledgeArtifact


@pytest.fixture
def ready_input():
    return ReadyForBazi(
        question="事业",
        profile=BirthProfile(year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_success_exact_tool_calls(ready_input, monkeypatch):
    """确保 Runtime 严格读取了计算工具和检索工具的双端 Artifact。"""
    import guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime

    adapter = LunarPythonBaziAdapter()
    real_chart = adapter.calculate(ready_input.profile)
    bazi_tool_msg = ToolMessage(
        content="{}", name="calculate_bazi", tool_call_id="1", artifact=real_chart
    )

    mock_res = RetrievalResult(
        rank=1,
        chunk_id="c1",
        document_id="d1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="T",
        source_type=SourceType.PROJECT_NOTE,
        section="S",
        text="T",
        rrf_score=0.9,
        rrf_rank=1,
        reranker_score=None,
        dense_rank=1,
        sparse_rank=1,
    )
    retrieval_tool_msg = ToolMessage(
        content="txt",
        name="retrieve_bazi_knowledge",
        tool_call_id="2",
        artifact=BaziKnowledgeArtifact(results=[mock_res]),
    )

    mock_agent = Mock()
    mock_agent.ainvoke = AsyncMock(
        return_value={
            "messages": [bazi_tool_msg, retrieval_tool_msg],
            "structured_response": BaziInterpretation(answer="成功获取解释", used_chunk_ids=["c1"]),
        }
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime, "create_agent", lambda **kw: mock_agent)
    monkeypatch.setattr(guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime, "build_chat_model", Mock())
    monkeypatch.setattr(guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime, "build_calculate_bazi_tool", Mock())
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime, "build_retrieve_bazi_knowledge_tool", Mock()
    )

    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(Mock(), Mock())
    result = await runtime.run(ready_input)

    assert result.answer == "成功获取解释"
    assert result.chart is real_chart
    assert len(result.citations) == 1
    assert result.citations[0].chunk_id == "c1"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_fails_on_zero_tool_calls(ready_input, monkeypatch):
    import guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime

    mock_agent = Mock()
    mock_agent.ainvoke = AsyncMock(
        return_value={
            "messages": [],
            "structured_response": BaziInterpretation(answer="我瞎猜的", used_chunk_ids=[]),
        }
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime, "create_agent", lambda **kw: mock_agent)
    monkeypatch.setattr(guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime, "build_chat_model", Mock())
    monkeypatch.setattr(guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime, "build_calculate_bazi_tool", Mock())
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime, "build_retrieve_bazi_knowledge_tool", Mock()
    )

    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(Mock(), Mock())
    with pytest.raises(BaziAgentInvariantError, match="必须只调用 1 次"):
        await runtime.run(ready_input)

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziAgentInput,
    BaziAgentResult,
    BaziBirthDataPatch,
    InvalidBaziInput,
    NeedUserInput,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.session import BaziTaskSession
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import Gender

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


@pytest.fixture
def mock_runtime():
    runtime = Mock()
    runtime.run = AsyncMock(return_value=Mock(spec=BaziAgentResult))
    return runtime


async def test_one_turn_completion(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(
        BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21), mock_runtime
    )
    assert isinstance(await session.evaluate(), NeedUserInput)
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 0

    res = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))
    assert isinstance(res, BaziAgentResult)
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 1


async def test_multi_turn_completion_and_question_preservation(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="感情怎样", year=1998), mock_runtime)
    res2 = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(month=7, day=21))
    assert isinstance(res2, NeedUserInput)

    res3 = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))
    assert isinstance(res3, BaziAgentResult)

    ready = mock_runtime.run.call_args.args[0]
    assert ready.question == "感情怎样"
    assert ready.profile.year == 1998


async def test_field_override_correction(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(
        BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=13), mock_runtime
    )
    await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_args.args[0].profile.hour == 14


async def test_invalid_patch_protects_state(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7), mock_runtime)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(month=17))
    assert session._current_input.month == 7


async def test_invalid_date_combination_recovery(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="算卦", year=2023, month=2), mock_runtime)
    assert isinstance(await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(day=29)), InvalidBaziInput)
    assert isinstance(await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(year=2024)), NeedUserInput)


async def test_completed_session_does_not_run_runtime_again(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(
        BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21), mock_runtime
    )
    result1 = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))

    result2 = await session.evaluate()
    assert result2 is result1
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 1

    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="已完成"):
        await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=15))

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziAgentInput,

    NeedUserInput,

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


@pytest.fixture
def mock_runtime():
    runtime = Mock()
    runtime.run = AsyncMock(return_value=Mock(spec=BaziAgentResult))
    return runtime


async def test_A01_complete_data_ready_for_bazi(mock_runtime):
    initial = BaziAgentInput(
        question="分析事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE
    )

    session = BaziTaskSession(initial, mock_runtime)

    assert isinstance(await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(month=7, day=21)), NeedUserInput)

    r4 = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))
    assert isinstance(r4, BaziAgentResult)
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 1


================================================================================
FILE: tests\agents\knowledge\test_agent_orchestration.py
================================================================================

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.agents.knowledge.agent import KnowledgeAgent
from guanchen_v2.agents.knowledge.models import KnowledgeAnswerDraft
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeAgentError, KnowledgeRetrievalError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    KnowledgeDomain,
    RetrievalFilter,
    RetrievalResult,
    SourceType,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.dense import DenseRetriever
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.service import RetrievalService
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.sparse import SparseRetriever
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient


def _create_result(chunk_id: str, rank: int) -> RetrievalResult:
    return RetrievalResult(
        rank=rank,
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id="doc1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="T",
        source_type=SourceType.PROJECT_NOTE,
        section="S",
        text="TXT",
        rrf_score=0.9,
        rrf_rank=rank,
        reranker_score=None,
        dense_rank=1,
        sparse_rank=2,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_retrieval_service_propagate_filters():
    """证明 Service 在调用 Dense/Sparse 时，毫无保留地下发了统一的 Filter，没自作主张。"""
    mock_dense = MagicMock(spec=DenseRetriever)
    mock_sparse = MagicMock(spec=SparseRetriever)
    mock_dense.search = AsyncMock(return_value=[])
    mock_rrf = MagicMock()
    mock_reranker = MagicMock()
    mock_assembler = MagicMock()
    mock_rrf.fuse.return_value = []

    service = RetrievalService(
        mock_dense,
        mock_sparse,
        mock_rrf,
        mock_reranker,
        mock_assembler,
        candidate_limit=5,
        result_limit=2,
    )

    filters = RetrievalFilter(domains=frozenset({KnowledgeDomain.BAZI}))
    await service.retrieve("Query", filters=filters)

    # 双方接收到的指令和过滤器必须一模一样
    mock_dense.search.assert_called_once_with("Query", limit=5, filters=filters)
    mock_sparse.search.assert_called_once_with("Query", limit=5, filters=filters)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_retrieval_service_limits_safeguards():
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeRetrievalError):
        RetrievalService(
            MagicMock(),
            MagicMock(),
            MagicMock(),
            MagicMock(),
            MagicMock(),
            candidate_limit=2,
            result_limit=5,
        )

    service = RetrievalService(
        MagicMock(), MagicMock(), MagicMock(), MagicMock(), MagicMock(), 5, 2
    )
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeRetrievalError, match="不能为空"):
        await service.retrieve("   ")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_empty_evidence_bypasses_llm_and_single_call():
    """无证不审测试：什么都没查到时，不但 LLM 不被唤醒，而且只进行了一次检索调用。"""
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)
    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[])
    mock_llm = MagicMock(spec=LLMClient)
    mock_llm.generate_structured = AsyncMock()

    agent = KnowledgeAgent(mock_service, mock_llm)
    resp = await agent.answer("什么是八字？")

    assert "无法据此作答" in resp.answer
    assert resp.evidence_sufficient is False
    # 【核心】：确保检索只被调用 1 次，没有诡异的自我反思重试
    mock_service.retrieve.assert_called_once_with(query="什么是八字？", filters=None)
    mock_llm.generate_structured.assert_not_called()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_zero_citations_discards_llm_hallucination():
    """【绝对核心】无引用必杀：如果模型交白卷却夹带私货，代码将彻底销毁其回答，返回防幻觉标准底牌！"""
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)
    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[_create_result("合法", 1)])

    mock_llm = MagicMock(spec=LLMClient)
    mock_llm.generate_structured = AsyncMock(
        return_value=KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
            answer="这是我自己背下来的知识，我要输出给你！",
            evidence_sufficient=False,
            used_chunk_ids=[],  # 重点：模型没有引用任何合法证据
        )
    )

    resp = await KnowledgeAgent(mock_service, mock_llm).answer("查")

    # 模型的私货被彻底物理抹除！
    assert resp.answer == "当前知识库中没有检索到足够依据，无法据此作答。"
    assert resp.evidence_sufficient is False
    assert len(resp.citations) == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_insufficient_evidence_enforces_stamp_securely():
    """部分引用防线：模型引用了证据，但又调皮捣蛋试图混淆前缀，代码强制盖章于最前方。"""
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)
    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[_create_result("合法_1", 1)])

    mock_llm = MagicMock(spec=LLMClient)
    mock_llm.generate_structured = AsyncMock(
        return_value=KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
            answer="哪怕这里有 现有检索证据不足以完整回答该问题 的子串，你也不能放过我。",
            evidence_sufficient=False,
            used_chunk_ids=["合法_1"],  # 重点：提供了合法引用，所以这是部分回答
        )
    )

    resp = await KnowledgeAgent(mock_service, mock_llm).answer("查")

    # 无论中间有多像，开头必定被盖上强制钢印
    assert resp.answer.startswith("现有检索证据不足以完整回答该问题。\n\n")
    assert "哪怕这里有" in resp.answer


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_llm_fabrication_fails_fast():
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)
    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[_create_result("合法_1", 1)])
    mock_llm = MagicMock(spec=LLMClient)

    mock_llm.generate_structured = AsyncMock(
        return_value=KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
            answer="编得很圆", evidence_sufficient=True, used_chunk_ids=["合法_1", "虚构的_999"]
        )
    )

    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeAgentError, match="未授权的证据编号"):
        await KnowledgeAgent(mock_service, mock_llm).answer("查询")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_knowledge_answer_draft_pydantic_invariants():
    """直接在模型层面拦截 '声称充分但交白卷' 的欺骗行为。"""
    from pydantic import ValidationError

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="必须至少提供一个实际引用的 chunk_id"):
        KnowledgeAnswerDraft(answer="满分答案", evidence_sufficient=True, used_chunk_ids=[])

    # 合法的不足情况
    draft = KnowledgeAnswerDraft(answer="我不知道", evidence_sufficient=False, used_chunk_ids=[])
    assert draft.evidence_sufficient is False


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\ingestion\test_chunker.py
================================================================================

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.chunker import KnowledgeDocumentChunker
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.errors import KnowledgeChunkingError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDocument, KnowledgeDomain, SourceType


def _create_doc(title: str, text: str) -> KnowledgeDocument:
    return KnowledgeDocument.from_parser(
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title=title,
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section="测试章节",
        text=text,
    )


def test_short_document_remains_one_chunk():
    """C1: 长度未越界的段落，必须 Bypass 保持绝对完整"""
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", "100个字符的正文。")
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)
    assert len(chunks) == 1
    assert chunks[0].text == "100个字符的正文。"
    # Metadata 全继承
    assert chunks[0].document_id == doc.document_id
    assert chunks[0].source_title == doc.source_title


def test_long_document_splits_with_metadata_inheritance():
    """C2: 长文档切分，且 Metadata 血缘完整继承"""
    text = "A" * 900
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)
    assert len(chunks) > 1
    for chunk in chunks:
        assert chunk.document_id == doc.document_id
        assert chunk.domain == doc.domain
        assert chunk.section == doc.section


def test_no_cross_document_contamination():
    """C3: 不同 Document 的切片绝不产生交叉感染"""
    doc_a = _create_doc("《书》", "A" * 900)
    doc_b = _create_doc("《测试》", "B" * 900)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)

    chunks = chunker.chunk_documents([doc_a, doc_b])
    a_chunks = [c for c in chunks if c.source_title == "《书》"]
    b_chunks = [c for c in chunks if c.source_title == "《测试》"]

    for c in a_chunks:
        assert "A" in c.text and "B" not in c.text
    for c in b_chunks:
        assert "B" in c.text and "A" not in c.text


def test_paragraph_aware_preference():
    """C4: 切片偏好验证，必须优先在段落处（双换行）下刀"""
    # 构造两个段落，单段均不到 100，总长超过 100
    text = "第一大段。" + ("A" * 60) + "\n\n第二大段。" + ("B" * 60)
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=100, chunk_overlap=10)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    # 因为在 \n\n 处切了，所以第一个块里绝对不该混入第二大段的内容
    assert "第二大段" not in chunks[0].text
    assert chunks[0].text.startswith("第一大段")


def test_deterministic_chunk_ids():
    """C5: 多次重试，生成的 ID 序列必须是恒定的指纹"""
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", "A" * 900)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)

    chunks_1 = chunker.chunk_document(doc)
    chunks_2 = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    ids_1 = [c.chunk_id for c in chunks_1]
    ids_2 = [c.chunk_id for c in chunks_2]
    assert ids_1 == ids_2


def test_whitespace_preservation_in_chunk_text():
    """C6: Chunk 储存的内容绝不被 Canonicalization 破坏段落和多余空格"""
    text = "第一段。   \n\n  第二段。"
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    chunk = chunker.chunk_document(doc)[0]

    assert "\n\n" in chunk.text
    assert "第一段。   \n\n  第二段。" == chunk.text


def test_ditiansui_multi_anchor_grouping():
    """C7: 滴天髓多锚点截断组装。不允许混杂！"""
    text = "【经文】A\n【原注】A解\n【经文】B\n【任氏曰】B解"
    doc = _create_doc("《滴天髓阐微》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    # 长度充裕，应当划分为两块，每块守着自己的锚
    assert len(chunks) == 2
    assert "【经文】A" in chunks[0].text and "【经文】B" not in chunks[0].text
    assert "【经文】B" in chunks[1].text and "【经文】A" not in chunks[1].text


def test_ditiansui_anchor_retained_after_fallback():
    """C8: 极其核心 - 当注解超长时，切断的残肢必须自带经文头部 context"""
    text = "【经文】何知其人富\n【任氏曰】" + ("释义" * 50)
    doc = _create_doc("《滴天髓阐微》", text)
    # 强行切断
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=50, chunk_overlap=5)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    assert len(chunks) > 1
    # 每一个被迫拆分的碎块，开头都必须带回原经文
    for chunk in chunks:
        assert chunk.text.startswith("【经文】何知其人富")
        assert len(chunk.text) > len("【经文】何知其人富")  # 不会产生 verse-only 孤立块


def test_empty_result_impossible():
    """C9: 输入有内容，绝对不能返回空"""
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", "哪怕只有一个字")
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    assert len(chunker.chunk_document(doc)) > 0


def test_invalid_config():
    """C10: 防止程序员乱填切分参数"""
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="必须大于 0"):
        KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=-1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="不能为负数"):
        KnowledgeDocumentChunker(chunk_overlap=-1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="严格小于"):
        KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=100, chunk_overlap=100)


def test_ditiansui_anchor_without_commentary_fails():
    """C11: 发现孤立经文锚点时 Fail Fast，决不静默产生 verse-only chunk"""
    # 只有经文锚点，没有任何后续说明正文
    text = "【经文】此句之后没有任何注解"
    doc = _create_doc("《滴天髓阐微》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)

    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeChunkingError, match="缺失关联释义"):
        chunker.chunk_document(doc)


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\ingestion\test_ingestion_service.py

from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings import BgeEmbeddingAdapter
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeTransactionError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.chunker import KnowledgeDocumentChunker
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.errors import KnowledgeChunkingError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.service import KnowledgeIngestionService
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDocument, KnowledgeDomain, SourceType
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.database import async_session, engine, init_db
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.models import KnowledgeChunkRow, KnowledgeDocumentRow

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
async def setup_database():
    """每次运行测试前清空环境，重建立 PGVector 架构表。"""
    await init_db()
    yield
    from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.models import Base

    async with engine.begin() as conn:
        await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.drop_all)


def _create_mock_adapter():
    """造一个假的大模型，返回确定性、数量相等的纯假 512 维列表。"""
    adapter = MagicMock(spec=BgeEmbeddingAdapter)
    adapter.embed_documents.side_effect = lambda chunks: [[0.5] * 512 for _ in chunks]
    return adapter


def _create_test_doc(text: str) -> KnowledgeDocument:
    return KnowledgeDocument.from_parser(
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="《渊海子平》",
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section="卷一 / 测试",
        text=text,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_empty_chunks_fails_fast():
    """S0 异常防线：不能悄无声息地跳过未产生 Chunk 的残破文档。"""
    chunker = MagicMock()
    chunker.chunk_document.return_value = []
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, _create_mock_adapter())

    doc = _create_test_doc("将被 Mock 掉的文本")
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeChunkingError, match="未产生任何 Chunk"):
        await service.ingest_document(doc)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_idempotent_ingestion_success_happy_path():
    """S1 首次完美摄入验证。确认 Document 和衍生 Chunk 被无损保存。"""
    doc = _create_test_doc("一段非常普通的初版测试文字，用于基础查验。")
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, _create_mock_adapter())

    await service.ingest_document(doc)

    async with async_session() as session:
        res_doc = await session.execute(
            select(KnowledgeDocumentRow).where(KnowledgeDocumentRow.document_id == doc.document_id)
        )
        assert res_doc.scalar_one().text == "一段非常普通的初版测试文字，用于基础查验。"

        res_chunks = await session.execute(
            select(KnowledgeChunkRow).where(KnowledgeChunkRow.document_id == doc.document_id)
        )
        saved_chunks = res_chunks.scalars().all()
        assert len(saved_chunks) == 1
        assert len(saved_chunks[0].embedding) == 512


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_idempotent_ingestion_identical_repeat_no_extra_rows():
    """S2 完全重复输入测试。必须维持稳定的一行记录和原样 Chunk，严禁行数膨胀！"""
    doc = _create_test_doc("不会变化的千古真理。")
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, _create_mock_adapter())

    await service.ingest_document(doc)
    await service.ingest_document(doc)

    async with async_session() as session:
        docs = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeDocumentRow))).scalars().all()
        assert len(docs) == 1
        chunks = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow))).scalars().all()
        assert len(chunks) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_idempotent_ingestion_text_update_replaces_chunks():
    """S3 高级幂等：旧历史必须全被物理除名，绝不留痕。"""
    # 故意设积极短的切割长度，迫使长文本产生大量的碎片 (Chunk)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=50, chunk_overlap=5)
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, _create_mock_adapter())

    # 模拟初版入库产生了多个切片
    doc_v1 = _create_test_doc("旧版文本一。" * 20)
    await service.ingest_document(doc_v1)

    async with async_session() as session:
        chunks_v1 = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow))).scalars().all()
        old_chunk_ids = {c.chunk_id for c in chunks_v1}
        assert len(old_chunk_ids) > 1

    # 同一个 document_id，但文案全部更新
    doc_v2 = _create_test_doc("全新的颠覆性文本二号，旧版全部作废！" * 15)
    await service.ingest_document(doc_v2)

    async with async_session() as session:
        docs = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeDocumentRow))).scalars().all()
        assert len(docs) == 1
        assert "全新的颠覆性文本二号" in docs[0].text

        chunks_v2 = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow))).scalars().all()
        new_chunk_ids = {c.chunk_id for c in chunks_v2}

        # 绝大部分旧 ID 必须全部被物理删除，绝不能出现新老交织的混合态
        assert len(new_chunk_ids) > 1
        assert not old_chunk_ids.intersection(new_chunk_ids)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_idempotent_ingestion_rollback_on_partial_failure():
    """S4 终极战役：基于 PostgreSQL 底层报错触发的物理级大回滚。"""
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    adapter = MagicMock(spec=BgeEmbeddingAdapter)

    # 设局：只要碰到含有特定词汇的 Chunk，就向数据库返回 PostgreSQL pgvector 无法忍受的 768 维瞎编向量！
    def fake_embed(chunks):
        if "造反" in chunks[0].text:
            return [[0.5] * 768 for _ in chunks]
        return [[0.5] * 512 for _ in chunks]

    adapter.embed_documents.side_effect = fake_embed
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, adapter)

    # Phase A：初版入库
    doc_v1 = _create_test_doc("绝不容篡改的正确初版。")
    await service.ingest_document(doc_v1)

    async with async_session() as session:
        old_chunks = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow))).scalars().all()
        old_chunk_ids = {c.chunk_id for c in old_chunks}

    # Phase B：模拟灾难现场
    # 此时：1. UPSERT 已完成; 2. DELETE 旧 Chunk 已完成; 3. INSERT 768维新 Chunk 惨遭 PostgreSQL 拒绝！
    doc_v2 = _create_test_doc("这版文本很正常，但我要在数据写入环节造反。")
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeTransactionError, match="知识文档幂等替换失败"):
        await service.ingest_document(doc_v2)

    # Phase C：探查战后现场（验证数据库原子性保障）
    async with async_session() as session:
        # Document 回退如初
        saved_doc = (
            await session.execute(
                select(KnowledgeDocumentRow).where(
                    KnowledgeDocumentRow.document_id == doc_v1.document_id
                )
            )
        ).scalar_one()
        assert saved_doc.text == "绝不容篡改的正确初版。"

        # 那些在事务中曾被 DELETE 的 Chunk 又满血复活了！
        saved_chunks = (
            (
                await session.execute(
                    select(KnowledgeChunkRow).where(
                        KnowledgeChunkRow.document_id == doc_v1.document_id
                    )
                )
            )
            .scalars()
            .all()
        )
        saved_chunk_ids = {c.chunk_id for c in saved_chunks}
        assert saved_chunk_ids == old_chunk_ids


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\ingestion\test_loader.py
